2026-10-30T02:27:08.8556046Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Serve the round-4 PP4 bundle (MegaMoE split accumulator, FA3 Prefill pipe, the fixed Decode candidates) with a fresh image

One image build and one PP4 serving run at concurrency 232 on top of the
9668.27 configuration (40964714831, image 0a02c4ab...1de4). There is no
profile pass in this run: the profile tag is not set anywhere in this
message.

The image must be rebuilt for two reasons:
- The DeepGEMM host series gained sm90-prefill-split-accum.patch.
- kernels/fa3-verify gained prefill-pipe.patch and the pipe row.
Provision rewrites the PP4 pins to this run's image, and
check-firmus-pp4-cpu.py accepts it through the sidecar naming this commit.

Changes and expected effect (power-cap factor in parentheses)

Prefill (PP0 period 117.2 / 116.9 / 120.1 ms at 9668.27)

1. MegaMoE four-stage-lean-split (786f2e1, DG_SM90_PREFILL_SPLIT_ACCUM=1).
   - What binds the K block: Genkai probes 40978170082 and 40980472965 of
     the FC1 mainloop model show it is the per-K-block FP32 carried-scale
     rescale (128 FMUL per warpgroup between WGMMA groups), not the shared
     memory port. Without the rescale the K block drops from 1406 to
     1090 clk.
   - What the variant does: each warpgroup issues its m64n256 accumulator
     as two m64n128 halves from the same stage, and rescales half h after
     waiting only for half h of the previous K block. This keeps the same
     products, K order and multipliers, so the output is bit-exact.
   - Probe ratios: FC1-shaped 0.944-0.948, FC2-shaped 0.924-0.926, under
     power-capped H100 clocks.
   - Registers: 224 via setmaxnreg, no spill; the other 22 instantiations
     have byte-identical SASS.
   - Expected 75-125 us per layer (0.6-1.0 of the probe ratio), so 1.8-3.0
     ms per stage period (-1.5..-2.6%).
   - Rejected on measurement: WGMMA with A loaded from global into
     registers (+34..+61%) and B multicast (no gain).

2. FA3 Prefill pipe row n192-direct-o-varlen-pipe (9445ec9, variant 224).
   - The first QK of the next M-tile and the last PV of the current one
     form one ping-pong step. Producer warps pass the tile coordinates, so
     there is no CTA-wide scheduler barrier per tile.
   - Output bytes and scales are equal to the pin.
   - Genkai 40981460928, 40983815241 and 40985249797: 0.9695 mean over
     nine paired trials; the pin against itself is 0.9996-1.0004.
   - It hides idle time rather than removing work, so -0.2..-0.35 ms per
     period (0.36-0.6).

3. One-pass Prefill MoE input pinned (4faea7b).
   - It won by 13.6% on all four GPUs in 40964714831 and is active in
     serving.
   - The 40 s selection leaves the Decode-GPU chain that gates worker start.

Decode (step about 34 ms; all three failed to run in 40964714831, fixed in 29d729c)

4. Fused TARGET_VERIFY MoE input: -0.19..-0.30 ms per step (1).
5. Two-KV-heads-per-CTA FA3 verify rows: 0..-1.7 ms per step (1). The
   read-granularity probe records TB/s at 128/256/512 B either way.
6. QKV BLOCK_M 192 x N80 tile for 129..192 rows: -0.3..-0.55 ms per step
   (1). N80 replaces N72, which the host's bn % 16 check rejected.

Totals: Prefill -1.7..-2.9%, Decode -1.5..-7%. The round-3 serving model
gives about 9,850-10,050 at c232; the best c is 228-232 for these factors,
with under 0.2% between them.

In-job gates (each falls back to the current winner or the pin)

- Provision: PP4 CPU gate on the new image (29 SGLang overlays) and
  check-firmus-image.sh (host series of 10 patches).
- Prefill GPUs, three workers in parallel:
  - select-firmus-prefill-megamoe.py: control, four-stage, lean, lean-pair
    and lean-split, bit-exact and faster in total. 240 s timeout, about
    80 s.
  - Then the FA3 pipe screen against the pinned row: byte equality per
    request over three replays plus the FP32 reference, and at least
    5 us/layer faster on every shape and all twelve GPUs. 175 s timeout,
    about 60-90 s.
- Decode GPUs, in sequence:
  - FA3 h2 rows: byte equality where the split equals the pin, at least
    1 us/layer on every GPU at 44/46/48 in both blocks.
  - Copy engine.
  - MoE input: equal residual, FP8 input, scales and top-k; max(1 us, 2%)
    per layer.
  - Dense BM192: bit-identical, at least 0.5 us at 176/184/192 rows.
  - Workers start about 4-4.5 min after job start, as in 40964714831.

Timeline: 40964714831 reached READY after 532 s with nsys-launched workers.
Expect READY about 500-530 s and Slurm elapsed about 14:00-14:40, more
than 2 minutes inside the 17-minute limit.

Judging checklist

- results/score.json: eligible, score against 9668.27.
- results/prefill-megamoe/selection.json: lean-split exactness on twelve
  GPUs and its total against lean.
- The FA3 pipe screen result and the FA3 line of source/prefill-kernels.env.
- results/decode-selection/{fa3,moe-input,dense}.json and the appended
  lines of source/decode-kernels.env; fa3.json read_probe TB/s.
- PP0 period per worker against 117.2 / 116.9 / 120.1 ms; telemetry SM
  clocks.
- Decode #running-req, ITL mean / p99 against 15.68 / 18.56 ms.
- freeze-gc.log and the absence of Decode output stalls.
